# Scene Atom Library — 500 reusable but executable scene patterns

## SA001
Setting: hospital corridor.
Scene objective: recognize a familiar object.
Entry state: A={position, desire, information}, B={position, desire, information}.
Spatial anchor: fixed landmark = {anchor_1}; primary axis = {axis_1}; foreground obstruction = {obstacle_1}.
Progression: 1) establish the ordinary geometry, 2) show one concrete intention, 3) introduce a friction cue, 4) allow a reaction that changes information, 5) escalate through character notices reflection, 6) force a choice, 7) end with a changed state rather than a generic suspense pose.
Camera grammar: start with {framing_A}, cut only when {visual_change} becomes legible; maintain subject entry/exit directions; use {movement_type} with a cause.
Performance: baseline {emotion_A}; trigger {trigger}; visible response {micro_action}; suppressed response {micro_action_2}; decision {choice}.
Sound: dialogue priority {dialogue_priority}, foreground effect {sfx}, environmental bed {ambience}, optional music {music_policy}.
Continuity output: actor positions, hand occupancy, gaze target, light source state, environment damage/state, audio state, unresolved objective.
Failure flags: teleport, prop duplication, axis flip, unexplained light source, action without preparation, reaction without cause.
